==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: README.md
==============================================================================
# AMH-OS

**AMH-OS** is an ESP32-S3 operating-system distribution built on Apache NuttX.

The repository contains an AMH-OS NSH command application, a provisional ESP32-S3 board profile, Windows/PyCharm workflow scripts, and documentation.

> **Hardware target:** ESP32-S3-WROOM development board. The board previously reported 16 MB flash and about 8 MB usable PSRAM. Its exact carrier-board model is not confirmed, so the upstream profile must be checked before flashing.

## Milestones

| Version | Scope | Implementation status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0.1 | NuttX boot, kernel startup, serial NSH | Upstream board configuration integrated; physical boot not yet tested |
| 0.2 | AMH-OS command system | AMH command dispatcher and subcommands added |
| 0.3 | CPU/memory monitoring | Uptime and optional heap statistics; live CPU utilization/PSRAM telemetry remains board-specific |
| 0.4 | Wi-Fi/network management | NuttX WAPI/network workflow and allow-listed native utility launcher added; driver configuration and hardware test pending |
| 0.5 | Filesystem/application management | VFS directory/capacity inspection and allow-listed utility launcher added |

## Architecture

- **Apache NuttX** provides the RTOS kernel, scheduler, drivers, networking stack, VFS and NSH.
- **AMH-OS** provides the branded command layer and device-management helpers.
- **No independent kernel or custom bootloader is claimed.** This is an NuttX-based distribution.

## Windows + PyCharm workflow

Windows is the primary host workflow. No Linux installation, Ubuntu installation, or WSL2 is required.

1. Install Git for Windows, Python 3, and Cygwin with the packages listed in `docs/GETTING_STARTED.md`.
2. Open this repository in PyCharm.
3. Run `scripts/amh.ps1` from the PyCharm PowerShell terminal:
   ```powershell
   Set-ExecutionPolicy -Scope Process Bypass
   .\scripts\amh.ps1 setup
   .\scripts\amh.ps1 bootstrap
   .\scripts\amh.ps1 configure
   .\scripts\amh.ps1 build
   .\scripts\amh.ps1 test
   ```

The PowerShell entry point handles the workflow, while Cygwin supplies the Bash/GNU build utilities required by NuttX's configuration and make system. This is a Windows development setup, not a Linux distribution. NuttX documents Cygwin as a Windows build environment; the native CMD build path is described upstream as less mature and not verified for every target.

The ESP32-S3 Xtensa Windows toolchain is downloaded and checksum-verified by the setup command. The exact Windows/Cygwin integration still requires a real build test; do not treat the build as validated until it succeeds locally.

## Wi-Fi hardware constraint

The ESP32-S3 supports **2.4 GHz Wi-Fi only** (IEEE 802.11 b/g/n). AMH-OS Wi-Fi testing must use a 2.4 GHz access point/network. It cannot join a 5 GHz-only network.

## NuttX build and board notes

The baseline profile is `esp32s3-devkit:nsh`. Wi-Fi driver and WAPI options must be enabled for the exact upstream NuttX revision. The exact carrier-board model is still unconfirmed.

## Safety and compatibility

- Building does not alter the board. Flashing replaces the firmware.
- Preserve the Wi-Fi Radar sketch source before flashing.
- The flash helper requires explicit board confirmation and typing `FLASH`; it does not request a full-chip erase.
- Do not enable secure-boot or flash-encryption eFuses in this development project.
- Do not assume all ESP32-S3-WROOM carrier boards have identical USB, UART, flash, PSRAM or pin configurations.
- Never commit Wi-Fi credentials.

## Validation

This repository contains source and build integration, not a verified prebuilt firmware image. No successful NuttX compile or physical-board test is claimed yet. See `docs/VALIDATION.md`.

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: Makefile
==============================================================================
SHELL := /bin/bash
.PHONY: help bootstrap configure build flash serial test clean
help:
	@echo "bootstrap | configure | build | test | flash PORT=/dev/ttyUSB0 AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED=YES | serial PORT=/dev/ttyUSB0"
bootstrap:
	bash ./scripts/bootstrap.sh
configure:
	bash ./scripts/configure.sh
build:
	bash ./scripts/build.sh
test:
	python3 ./tests/check_project.py
flash:
	@test -n "$(PORT)" || (echo "Usage: make flash PORT=/dev/ttyUSB0 AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED=YES"; exit 2)
	@test "$(AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED)" = "YES" || (echo "Set AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED=YES only after verifying the exact board."; exit 2)
	AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED="$(AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED)" bash ./scripts/flash.sh "$(PORT)"
serial:
	@test -n "$(PORT)" || (echo "Usage: make serial PORT=/dev/ttyUSB0"; exit 2)
	bash ./scripts/serial.sh "$(PORT)"
clean:
	@test ! -d third_party/nuttx || $(MAKE) -C third_party/nuttx distclean

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: scripts\amh.ps1
==============================================================================
param(
    [Parameter(Position = 0)]
    [ValidateSet("help", "setup", "bootstrap", "configure", "build", "test", "flash", "serial")]
    [string]$Command = "help",

    [string]$Port,

    [switch]$ConfirmBoard
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$Root = (Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..")).Path
$ToolchainRoot = Join-Path $Root "tools\xtensa-esp-elf"
$ToolchainBin = Join-Path $ToolchainRoot "bin"
$ExistingCompiler = Get-ChildItem -Path $ToolchainRoot -Filter "xtensa-esp-elf-gcc.exe" -File -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1
if ($ExistingCompiler) { $ToolchainBin = $ExistingCompiler.Directory.FullName }
$VenvPython = Join-Path $Root ".venv-windows\Scripts\python.exe"
$Bash = if ($env:CYGWIN_BASH) { $env:CYGWIN_BASH } else { "C:\cygwin64\bin\bash.exe" }

function Show-Help {
    @"
AMH-OS Windows / PyCharm commands

  .\scripts\amh.ps1 setup
  .\scripts\amh.ps1 bootstrap
  .\scripts\amh.ps1 configure
  .\scripts\amh.ps1 build
  .\scripts\amh.ps1 test
  .\scripts\amh.ps1 serial -Port COM16
  .\scripts\amh.ps1 flash -Port COM16 -ConfirmBoard

setup      Download/check the Windows Xtensa toolchain and create Python tools venv.
bootstrap  Clone Apache NuttX and NuttX Apps into third_party.
configure Configure NuttX and register the AMH app using Cygwin Bash.
build      Build NuttX using Cygwin make and the Windows Xtensa toolchain.
test       Run repository static checks with Windows Python.
serial     Open the COM port at 115200 baud.
flash      Explicitly confirm the board, then flash the built firmware.

No Linux, Ubuntu, or WSL2 installation is used. Cygwin is required because
NuttX's configure/build system uses Bash and GNU build utilities.
"@ | Write-Host
}

function Get-CygwinBash {
    if (-not (Test-Path $Bash)) {
        throw "Cygwin Bash not found at '$Bash'. Install Cygwin or set CYGWIN_BASH to the full path of bash.exe."
    }
    return (Resolve-Path $Bash).Path
}

function Invoke-Cygwin([string]$BashCommand) {
    $bashPath = Get-CygwinBash
    $cygpath = Join-Path (Split-Path $bashPath) "cygpath.exe"
    if (-not (Test-Path $cygpath)) {
        throw "cygpath.exe is missing beside Cygwin bash.exe. Install the Cygwin coreutils package."
    }
    $cygRoot = (& $cygpath -u $Root).Trim()
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or -not $cygRoot) { throw "Could not convert project path for Cygwin." }
    $pythonScripts = Join-Path $Root ".venv-windows\\Scripts"
    $env:PATH = "$ToolchainBin;$pythonScripts;$env:PATH"
    & $bashPath -lc "cd '$cygRoot' && $BashCommand"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "Cygwin command failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE." }
}

switch ($Command) {
    "help" {
        Show-Help
    }
    "setup" {
        $version = "14.2.0_20241119"
        $archiveName = "xtensa-esp-elf-$version-x86_64-w64-mingw32.zip"
        $url = "https://github.com/espressif/crosstool-NG/releases/download/esp-$version/$archiveName"
        $expectedSha256 = "62ae704777d73c30689efff6e81178632a1ca44d1a2d60f4621eb997e040e028"
        $downloadDir = Join-Path $Root "downloads"
        $archive = Join-Path $downloadDir $archiveName
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $downloadDir | Out-Null
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $ToolchainRoot | Out-Null

        if (-not (Get-ChildItem -Path $ToolchainRoot -Filter "xtensa-esp-elf-gcc.exe" -File -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1)) {
            Write-Host "Downloading Espressif Xtensa ESP32-S3 Windows toolchain..."
            Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -OutFile $archive
            $actual = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $archive).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
            if ($actual -ne $expectedSha256) {
                Remove-Item $archive -Force
                throw "Toolchain checksum mismatch. Download removed."
            }
            Expand-Archive -Path $archive -DestinationPath $ToolchainRoot -Force
        }

        $compiler = Get-ChildItem -Path $ToolchainRoot -Filter "xtensa-esp-elf-gcc.exe" -File -Recurse | Select-Object -First 1
        if (-not $compiler) { throw "Toolchain extraction did not contain xtensa-esp-elf-gcc.exe." }
        $ToolchainBin = $compiler.Directory.FullName
        $env:PATH = "$ToolchainBin;$env:PATH"
        & $compiler.FullName --version
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "Xtensa compiler did not start successfully." }

        if (-not (Test-Path $VenvPython)) {
            python -m venv (Join-Path $Root ".venv-windows")
        }
        & $VenvPython -m pip install --upgrade pip
        & $VenvPython -m pip install "esptool>=4.8.1" "pyserial>=3.5"
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "Could not install esptool and pyserial in the project virtual environment." }
        Write-Host "Windows toolchain and Python utilities are prepared."
        Write-Host "Cygwin must still be installed separately with the packages in docs/GETTING_STARTED.md."
    }
    "bootstrap" {
        $thirdParty = Join-Path $Root "third_party"
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $thirdParty | Out-Null
        if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $thirdParty "nuttx\.git"))) {
            git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/apache/nuttx.git (Join-Path $thirdParty "nuttx")
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "NuttX clone failed." }
        } else { Write-Host "NuttX checkout already exists; leaving it unchanged." }
        if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $thirdParty "apps\.git"))) {
            git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/apache/nuttx-apps.git (Join-Path $thirdParty "apps")
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "NuttX Apps clone failed." }
        } else { Write-Host "NuttX Apps checkout already exists; leaving it unchanged." }
    }
    "configure" {
        if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $Root "third_party\nuttx\.git"))) {
            throw "Run .\scripts\amh.ps1 bootstrap first."
        }
        Invoke-Cygwin "bash scripts/configure.sh"
    }
    "build" {
        if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $Root "third_party\nuttx\.config"))) {
            throw "Run .\scripts\amh.ps1 configure first."
        }
        if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $ToolchainBin "xtensa-esp-elf-gcc.exe"))) {
            throw "Run .\scripts\amh.ps1 setup first."
        }
        Invoke-Cygwin "bash scripts/build.sh"
    }
    "test" {
        python (Join-Path $Root "tests\check_project.py")
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "Project checks failed." }
    }
    "serial" {
        if (-not $Port) { throw "Specify -Port COM16 (replace with the COM port shown in Device Manager)." }
        if (-not (Test-Path $VenvPython)) { throw "Run .\scripts\amh.ps1 setup first to install pyserial." }
        & $VenvPython -m serial.tools.miniterm $Port 115200
    }
    "flash" {
        if (-not $Port) { throw "Specify -Port COM16 (replace with the COM port shown in Device Manager)." }
        if (-not $ConfirmBoard) { throw "Flash refused. Add -ConfirmBoard only after verifying the exact ESP32-S3 carrier-board model and flash settings." }
        if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $Root "third_party\nuttx\nuttx"))) { throw "Built NuttX ELF not found. Run build first." }
        Write-Warning "Flashing replaces the firmware currently on $Port. Preserve the Wi-Fi Radar source first."
        $typed = Read-Host "Type FLASH to continue"
        if ($typed -cne "FLASH") { throw "Flash cancelled." }
        $env:AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED = "YES"
        try {
            Invoke-Cygwin "AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED=YES bash scripts/flash.sh $Port"
        } finally {
            Remove-Item Env:AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        }
    }
}

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: scripts\bootstrap.sh
==============================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
mkdir -p "$ROOT/third_party"
cd "$ROOT/third_party"
if [[ ! -d nuttx/.git ]]; then
  git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/apache/nuttx.git nuttx
else
  echo "NuttX checkout already exists; leaving it unchanged."
fi
if [[ ! -d apps/.git ]]; then
  git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/apache/nuttx-apps.git apps
else
  echo "NuttX apps checkout already exists; leaving it unchanged."
fi
echo "Upstream sources ready. Next: ./scripts/configure.sh"

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: scripts\configure.sh
==============================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
NUTTX="$ROOT/third_party/nuttx"
APPS="$ROOT/third_party/apps"
APP_DST="$APPS/examples/amh"

if [[ ! -x "$NUTTX/tools/configure.sh" || ! -d "$APPS/examples" ]]; then
    echo "Run bash scripts/bootstrap.sh first." >&2
    exit 1
fi

# Install the AMH application files into NuttX Apps.
mkdir -p "$APP_DST"

cp "$ROOT/apps/amh/amh_main.c" "$APP_DST/amh_main.c"
cp "$ROOT/apps/amh/Kconfig" "$APP_DST/Kconfig"
cp "$ROOT/apps/amh/Make.defs" "$APP_DST/Make.defs"
cp "$ROOT/apps/amh/Makefile" "$APP_DST/Makefile"

# Normalize the registration files to LF.
sed -i 's/\r$//' "$APPS/examples/Make.defs"
sed -i 's/\r$//' "$APPS/examples/Kconfig"

# Remove any legacy explicit AMH Make.defs registration.
# NuttX already discovers examples/*/Make.defs through its wildcard.
sed -i '/^include examples\/amh\/Make\.defs$/d' "$APPS/examples/Make.defs"

# Ensure the standard automatic discovery rule is present.
if ! grep -qF 'include $(wildcard $(APPDIR)/examples/*/Make.defs)' "$APPS/examples/Make.defs"; then
    echo "Expected NuttX examples Make.defs wildcard was not found." >&2
    exit 1
fi

# Register the AMH Kconfig file exactly once.
AMH_KCONFIG_SOURCE="source \"$(cygpath -u "$APP_DST/Kconfig")\""
if ! grep -qF "$AMH_KCONFIG_SOURCE" "$APPS/examples/Kconfig"; then
    sed -i '/source "examples\/amh\/Kconfig"/d' "$APPS/examples/Kconfig"
    printf '\n%s\n' "$AMH_KCONFIG_SOURCE" >> "$APPS/examples/Kconfig"
fi

cd "$NUTTX"

# Configure the ESP32-S3 NSH baseline.
./tools/configure.sh esp32s3-devkit:nsh

# Enable AMH and required NSH options.
for option in \
    CONFIG_EXAMPLES_AMH \
    CONFIG_LIBC_EXECFUNCS \
    CONFIG_BUILTIN \
    CONFIG_NSH_BUILTIN_APPS \
    CONFIG_SCHED_WAITPID
do
    if ! grep -q "^${option}=y$" .config; then
        printf '%s=y\n' "$option" >> .config
    fi
done

# Resolve Kconfig dependencies and generate the final configuration.
make olddefconfig

echo "Configured esp32s3-devkit:nsh with the AMH application."
echo "Wi-Fi driver and WAPI options must still be enabled separately."
echo "Review flash and PSRAM settings before building or flashing."

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: scripts\build.sh
==============================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
NUTTX="$ROOT/third_party/nuttx"
if [[ ! -f "$NUTTX/.config" ]]; then
  echo "No configuration. Run ./scripts/configure.sh first." >&2
  exit 1
fi
for tool in xtensa-esp-elf-gcc xtensa-esp-elf-ld; do
  command -v "$tool" >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
    echo "Missing $tool. Install the Espressif Xtensa toolchain and update PATH." >&2
    exit 1
  }
done
JOBS="$(getconf _NPROCESSORS_ONLN 2>/dev/null || echo 2)"
cd "$NUTTX"
make -j"$JOBS"
echo "Build finished. Inspect generated images before flashing."

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: scripts\flash.sh
==============================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
NUTTX="$ROOT/third_party/nuttx"
if [[ "${AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED:-}" != "YES" ]]; then
  echo "REFUSED: confirm the exact carrier-board model first (AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED=YES)." >&2
  exit 2
fi
if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
  echo "Usage: AMH_BOARD_CONFIRMED=YES bash scripts/flash.sh /dev/ttyUSB0" >&2
  exit 2
fi
PORT="$1"
if [[ ! -f "$NUTTX/nuttx" ]]; then
  echo "Firmware ELF not found. Run the build first." >&2
  exit 1
fi
echo "WARNING: flashing replaces the firmware currently on $PORT."
echo "No full-chip erase is requested. Verify board, port and flash settings."
read -r -p "Type FLASH to continue: " CONFIRM
[[ "$CONFIRM" == "FLASH" ]] || { echo "Cancelled."; exit 1; }
cd "$NUTTX"
make -j"$(getconf _NPROCESSORS_ONLN 2>/dev/null || echo 2)" flash ESPTOOL_PORT="$PORT" ESPTOOL_BINDIR=./

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: boards\esp32s3\README.md
==============================================================================
# ESP32-S3 board profile

Initial NuttX profile: `esp32s3-devkit:nsh`. This is provisional because the exact carrier-board model is not confirmed.

## Console
- UART0 / COM-UART USB connector
- 115200 baud, 8 data bits, no parity, 1 stop bit
- Expected prompt: `nsh>`

Before flashing, confirm the module marking, carrier-board model, USB/UART wiring, flash/PSRAM part and NuttX flash configuration. Local Arduino diagnostics previously reported 16 MB flash and about 8 MB usable PSRAM; these values must be checked independently under NuttX.

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: apps\amh\Kconfig
==============================================================================
config EXAMPLES_AMH
	bool "AMH-OS command suite"
	default y
	depends on NSH_LIBRARY
	---help---
		Build the AMH-OS user-space command suite. Available at the NSH prompt:
		  nsh> amh help

config EXAMPLES_AMH_PROGNAME
	string "AMH-OS program name"
	default "amh"
	depends on EXAMPLES_AMH

config EXAMPLES_AMH_PRIORITY
	int "AMH-OS task priority"
	default 100
	depends on EXAMPLES_AMH

config EXAMPLES_AMH_STACKSIZE
	int "AMH-OS task stack size"
	default 4096
	depends on EXAMPLES_AMH

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: docs\HARDWARE.md
==============================================================================
# Hardware compatibility and flash precautions

## Known test-board facts
- ESP32-S3 (QFN56), dual-core Xtensa LX7, up to 240 MHz.
- ESP32-S3-WROOM module family.
- 16 MB flash detected by Arduino diagnostic.
- Approximately 8 MB usable PSRAM detected by Arduino diagnostic.
- Two USB-C connectors labelled COM/UART and USB; COM/UART used for serial upload/monitor.
- Wi-Fi is **2.4 GHz only**, IEEE 802.11 b/g/n. 5 GHz Wi-Fi is not supported by ESP32-S3.

The exact carrier-board manufacturer/model and USB bridge are not confirmed.

## Initial upstream profile
Apache NuttX `esp32s3-devkit:nsh`. This is a baseline, not proof that every third-party ESP32-S3-WROOM board is electrically identical.

## Flash size
Do not blindly copy the NuttX documentation's example `-fs 4MB` setting onto a board detected with 16 MB flash. Review the generated image settings and selected board config before flashing.

## Non-destructive policy
- No automatic full-chip erase.
- No secure-boot or flash-encryption eFuse commands.
- No hardcoded Wi-Fi credentials.
- Flashing is a separate explicit action.
- Preserve the Wi-Fi Radar sketch source before replacing its firmware.

==============================================================================
  PROJECT FILE: docs\ARCHITECTURE.md
==============================================================================
# AMH-OS architecture

AMH-OS is an ESP32-S3 operating-system distribution built on Apache NuttX. It is not a new kernel written from scratch. NuttX supplies hardware startup/boot integration, kernel, scheduler, drivers, VFS, networking and NuttShell (NSH). AMH-OS supplies its identity, command application, board profile, setup scripts and device-management conventions.

## Layers
1. ESP32-S3 ROM and Espressif startup flow used by the NuttX port.
2. NuttX kernel: scheduling, synchronization, memory management and drivers.
3. NuttX services: NSH, VFS, network stack and configured utilities.
4. AMH command app: the `amh` NSH command and subcommands.
5. Future NuttX applications.

## Milestones
- 0.1: upstream `esp32s3-devkit:nsh`, UART0 console at 115200 baud.
- 0.2: `amh` command dispatcher with help and subcommands.
- 0.3: uptime and heap statistics where exposed by the selected NuttX configuration; chip/core/nominal clock identification. Runtime CPU utilization and external PSRAM accounting require board-specific telemetry and are not fabricated.
- 0.4: NuttX network/Wi-Fi stack and WAPI utilities. Driver options must match the checked-out NuttX revision.
- 0.5: filesystem inspection using VFS APIs and an allow-listed application launcher.

The first release registers one stable NSH builtin, `amh`, with subcommands. This avoids fragile patches to the upstream NSH parser.

Upstream NuttX and NuttX apps are separate checkouts under `third_party/`. Credentials and generated firmware binaries must never be committed.
